# Off-GPU inventory

**GPU 100% target: every site below ticked off, nothing off the GPU.**

Generated by `node scripts/off-gpu-scan.mjs --doc` (`npm run offgpu`); do not edit by hand. The checklist at the end is regenerated from the scan, so a site leaves it when it moves to the GPU.
No percentage blocks a push or a deploy; the honest score is printed and the target is 100%. What each category means is at the top of the script.

**2684/4063 on GPU (66% honest, counting 796 class-write and timer/observer sites; target 100%), 1379 off**.
